			PopupText.NewText(PopupTextContext.RegularItemPickup, newItem, base.Center, newItem.stack, noStack: false, settings.LongText);
		}
		if (!settings.NoCoinMerge)
		{
			DoCoins(i);
		}
		AchievementsHelper.NotifyItemPickup(this, returnItem);
		if (whoAmI == Main.myPlayer && newItem.type == 5095)
		{
			LucyAxeMessage.Create(LucyAxeMessage.MessageSource.PickedUp, base.Top, new Vector2(0f, -7f));
		}
		settings.HandlePostAction(inventory[i]);
		return true;
	}

	public void PlaceThing(bool doPlacementAction, ref ItemCheckContext context)
	{
		if (itemTime == 0)
		{
			dontConsumeWand = false;
		}
		PlaceThing_Paintbrush();
		PlaceThing_PaintRoller();
		PlaceThing_PaintScrapper();
		PlaceThing_CannonBall();
		PlaceThing_XMasTreeTops();
		PlaceThing_ItemInExtractinator(ref context);
		PlaceThing_LockChest();
		if (!noBuilding)
		{
			PlaceThing_Tiles(doPlacementAction);
			PlaceThing_Walls();
		}
	}

	{
		if (inventory[selectedItem].createWall < 0 || !IsInTileInteractionRange(tileTargetX, tileTargetY, TileReachCheckSettings.Simple, inventory[selectedItem].tileBoost + blockRange))
		{

	{

		{

	private void PlaceThing_PaintScrapper()
	{
		if (ItemID.Sets.IsPaintScraper[inventory[selectedItem].type] && IsInTileInteractionRange(tileTargetX, tileTargetY, TileReachCheckSettings.Simple, inventory[selectedItem].tileBoost + blockRange))
		{
			int num = tileTargetX;

			if (Main.tile[num, num2] != null)
			{
				PlaceThing_PaintScrapper_TryScrapping(num, num2);
				PlaceThing_PaintScrapper_LongMoss(num, num2);
			}
		}
	}

	private void PlaceThing_PaintScrapper_LongMoss(int x, int y)
	{
		if (Main.tile[x, y].type != 184)
		{
			return;
		}
		cursorItemIconEnabled = true;
		if (!ItemTimeIsZero || itemAnimation <= 0 || !controlUseItem)
		{
			return;
		}
		_ = Main.tile[x, y].type;
		int frameX = Main.tile[x, y].frameX;
		WorldGen.KillTile(x, y);
		if (Main.tile[x, y].active())
		{
			return;
		}
		ApplyItemTime(inventory[selectedItem]);
		if (Main.netMode == 1)
		{
			NetMessage.SendData(17, -1, -1, null, 0, x, y);
		}
		if (Main.rand.Next(9) == 0)
		{
			int type = 4349 + frameX / 22;
			switch (frameX / 22)
			{
			case 6:
				type = 4377;
				break;
			case 7:
				type = 4378;
				break;
			case 8:
				type = 4389;
				break;
			case 9:
				type = 5127;
				break;
			case 10:
				type = 5128;
				break;
			}
			int number = Item.NewItem(WorldGen.GetItemSource_FromTileBreak(x, y), x * 16, y * 16, 16, 16, type);
			NetMessage.SendData(21, -1, -1, null, number, 1f);
		}
	}

	private void PlaceThing_PaintScrapper_TryScrapping(int x, int y)
	{
		Tile tile = Main.tile[x, y];
		if ((0u | ((tile.wall > 0 && (tile.wallColor() > 0 || tile.invisibleWall() || tile.fullbrightWall())) ? 1u : 0u) | ((tile.active() && (tile.color() > 0 || tile.invisibleBlock() || tile.fullbrightBlock())) ? 1u : 0u)) == 0)
		{
			return;
		}
		cursorItemIconEnabled = true;
		if (ItemTimeIsZero && itemAnimation > 0 && controlUseItem)
		{
			if (WorldGen.paintTile(x, y, 0, broadCast: true) || WorldGen.paintCoatTile(x, y, 0, broadcast: true))
			{
				ApplyItemTime(inventory[selectedItem], tileSpeed);
			}
			else if (WorldGen.paintWall(x, y, 0, broadCast: true) || WorldGen.paintCoatWall(x, y, 0, broadcast: true))
			{
				ApplyItemTime(inventory[selectedItem], wallSpeed);
			}
		}
	}

	private void PlaceThing_PaintRoller()
	{
		if ((inventory[selectedItem].type != 1072 && inventory[selectedItem].type != 1544) || !IsInTileInteractionRange(tileTargetX, tileTargetY, TileReachCheckSettings.Simple, inventory[selectedItem].tileBoost + blockRange))
		{
			return;
		}
		int num = tileTargetX;
		int num2 = tileTargetY;
		if (Main.tile[num, num2] != null && Main.tile[num, num2].wall > 0)
		{
			cursorItemIconEnabled = true;
			if (ItemTimeIsZero && itemAnimation > 0 && controlUseItem)
			{
				TryPainting(num, num2, paintingAWall: true);
			}
		}
	}

	private void PlaceThing_Paintbrush()
	{
		if ((inventory[selectedItem].type != 1071 && inventory[selectedItem].type != 1543) || !IsInTileInteractionRange(tileTargetX, tileTargetY, TileReachCheckSettings.Simple, inventory[selectedItem].tileBoost + blockRange))

	public Item FindPaintOrCoating()
	{
		for (int i = 54; i < 58; i++)

			{

		}

	}

	private void TryPainting(int x, int y, bool paintingAWall = false, bool applyItemAnimation = true)

			if (item.paint != 0)
			{
				ApplyPaint(x, y, paintingAWall, applyItemAnimation, item);
			}
		}
	}

	private void ApplyCoating(int x, int y, bool paintingAWall, bool applyItemAnimation, Item targetItem)
	{

				if (applyItemAnimation)
				{
					ApplyItemTime(inventory[selectedItem], wallSpeed);
				}
			}
		}
		else if (WorldGen.paintCoatTile(x, y, paintCoating, broadcast: true))
		{
			targetItem.stack--;
			if (targetItem.stack <= 0)
			{
				targetItem.SetDefaults(0);
			}
			if (applyItemAnimation)
			{
				ApplyItemTime(inventory[selectedItem], tileSpeed);
			}
		}
	}

So the roller doesn't paint a row at once; it paints one wall tile per use, but because it works on walls it's faster/easier for large surfaces? Actually the vanilla roller paints a single tile per use too. The "row" idea: hmm. Let me verify by checking the original 1.4.4 code... In vanilla Player.cs:

```csharp
private void PlaceThing_PaintRoller()
{
    if ((inventory[selectedItem].type == 1072 || inventory[selectedItem].type == 1544) && IsInTileInteractionRange(tileTargetX, tileTargetY, TileReachCheckSettings.Simple, inventory[selectedItem].tileBoost + blockRange))
    {
        int num = tileTargetX;
        int num2 = tileTargetY;
        if (Main.tile[num, num2] != null && Main.tile[num, num2].wall > 0)
        {
            cursorItemIconEnabled = true;
            if (ItemTimeIsZero && itemAnimation > 0 && controlUseItem)
                TryPainting(num, num2, paintingAWall: true);
        }
    }
}
```
Yes — matches. So the roller paints one wall tile per item-time tick, same as brush but for walls.

Consumption: 1 paint per painted tile (only if the color actually changes — WorldGen.paintTile returns false if same color, so no consumption when re-painting same color). ApplyCoating consumes 1 if the coating changed. Note TryPainting may consume 2 per tile if the held paint item has BOTH paint and paintCoating — but no item has both (each item has either paint or paintCoating).

Note ApplyPaint checks `Main.tile[x, y].wallColor() != paint` before calling paintWall — an extra guard so no consumption if the same color.

Note: for coating, ApplyCoating doesn't pre-check; WorldGen.paintCoatTile returns false if already coated, so no consumption.

Important: no HasAmmo/PickAmmo for paint. It uses FindPaintOrCoating() which searches ammo slots (54-57) first, then the whole inventory (0-57). Paint items fit ammo slots via FitsAmmoSlot (Item.cs:1351).

# 调研报告：泰拉瑞亚 1.4.5.6（反编译源码）玩家涂刷油漆交互逻辑

先给 4 个**前提更正**（与提问中的假设不符，均已在源码核实）：

1. **油漆三件套不是 useStyle=13**。`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/Terarria1456/Terraria.ID/ItemUseStyleID.cs:31` 定义 `public const int Rapier = 13;`；而 Paintbrush/PaintRoller/PaintScraper 在 `Item.cs` SetDefaults 里全部是 `useStyle = 1`（Swing）。`Player.cs:50423` 的 `else if (sItem.useStyle == 13)` 分支只是 Rapier 挥刺的持械动画，与涂刷无关。
2. **Item 没有 `paintWall` 字段**。`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/Terarria1456/Terraria/Item.cs:118-120` 只有 `public byte paint;` 和 `public byte paintCoating;`。tile 通道 vs wall 通道由**手持哪把工具**决定，不由物品 paint 值决定，也不看 `controlUseTile`。
3. **SmartCursor 不在 `Terraria/SmartCursor.cs`**，而是 `~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/Terarria1456/Terraria.GameContent/SmartCursorHelper.cs`（目录名带点的反编译产物）；本版本没有 `SmartCursorSettingsHandler` 类，`Player.SmartCursorSettings` 只有 `SmartBlocksEnabled / SmartAxeAfterPickaxe / SmartCursorHoldCanReleaseMidUse`（`Player.cs:322-329`）。
4. **RedPaint 是 1073 不是 1071**（1071 是 Paintbrush）；**没有 IlluminantPaint**——1.4.4 起被 `GlowPaint`(4668，本地化名 "Illuminant Coating") 和 `EchoCoating`(5344) 两种"涂层(coating)"取代。

---

## 1. Player.cs 中油漆的主逻辑段

### 1.0 调用链

`Player.cs:42926`（`ItemCheck_OwnerOnlyCode` 内）→ `PlaceThing` → 三个油漆方法。注意油漆调用**不在** `if (!noBuilding)` 之内（即 noBuilding 不禁涂刷）。

`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/Terarria1456/Terraria/Player.cs:38917-38935`
```csharp
	public void PlaceThing(bool doPlacementAction, ref ItemCheckContext context)
	{
		if (itemTime == 0)
		{
			dontConsumeWand = false;
		}
		PlaceThing_Paintbrush();
		PlaceThing_PaintRoller();
		PlaceThing_PaintScrapper();
		PlaceThing_CannonBall();
		PlaceThing_XMasTreeTops();
		PlaceThing_ItemInExtractinator(ref context);
		PlaceThing_LockChest();
		if (!noBuilding)
		{
			PlaceThing_Tiles(doPlacementAction);
			PlaceThing_Walls();
		}
	}
```
语义：每帧使用物品时先依次尝试刷子/滚刷/刮刀三个专用分支，再走普通放砖放墙逻辑。

### 1.1 (a) 刷子 Paintbrush —— 涂单个 tile

`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/Terarria1456/Terraria/Player.cs:41254-41270`
```csharp
	private void PlaceThing_Paintbrush()
	{
		if ((inventory[selectedItem].type != 1071 && inventory[selectedItem].type != 1543) || !IsInTileInteractionRange(tileTargetX, tileTargetY, TileReachCheckSettings.Simple, inventory[selectedItem].tileBoost + blockRange))
		{
			return;
		}
		int num = tileTargetX;
		int num2 = tileTargetY;
		if (Main.tile[num, num2] != null && Main.tile[num, num2].active())
		{
			cursorItemIconEnabled = true;
			if (ItemTimeIsZero && itemAnimation > 0 && controlUseItem)
			{
				TryPainting(num, num2);
			}
		}
	}
```
语义：手持 1071(Paintbrush) 或 1543(SpectrePaintbrush)、在交互距离内、目标格有 active 方块时，每个 itemTime 周期对**当前光标格**（仅一格）执行 `TryPainting`，走 tile 通道（默认 `paintingAWall: false`）。

### 1.2 (b) 滚刷 PaintRoller —— 判定是"墙存在"，不是"涂一排"

`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/Terarria1456/Terraria/Player.cs:41236-41252`
```csharp
	private void PlaceThing_PaintRoller()
	{
		if ((inventory[selectedItem].type != 1072 && inventory[selectedItem].type != 1544) || !IsInTileInteractionRange(tileTargetX, tileTargetY, TileReachCheckSettings.Simple, inventory[selectedItem].tileBoost + blockRange))
		{
			return;
		}
		int num = tileTargetX;
		int num2 = tileTargetY;
		if (Main.tile[num, num2] != null && Main.tile[num, num2].wall > 0)
		{
			cursorItemIconEnabled = true;
			if (ItemTimeIsZero && itemAnimation > 0 && controlUseItem)
			{
				TryPainting(num, num2, paintingAWall: true);
			}
		}
	}
```
语义：手持 1072(PaintRoller)/1544(SpectrePaintRoller) 且目标格有墙（`wall > 0`）时，每个 itemTime 周期对**同一格**的墙执行 `TryPainting(..., paintingAWall: true)`。**滚刷并没有"一次涂一排"的判定**——它和刷子一样一次只处理光标那一格，只是作用对象换成墙（itemTime=10 帧一次，所以拖动时连续刷）。

### 1.3 (c) 刮刀 PaintScraper —— 清除 + 剃长苔藓

`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/Terarria1456/Terraria/Player.cs:41152-41164`
```csharp
	private void PlaceThing_PaintScrapper()
	{
		if (ItemID.Sets.IsPaintScraper[inventory[selectedItem].type] && IsInTileInteractionRange(tileTargetX, tileTargetY, TileReachCheckSettings.Simple, inventory[selectedItem].tileBoost + blockRange))
		{
			int num = tileTargetX;
			int num2 = tileTargetY;
			if (Main.tile[num, num2] != null)
			{
				PlaceThing_PaintScrapper_TryScrapping(num, num2);
				PlaceThing_PaintScrapper_LongMoss(num, num2);
			}
		}
	}
```

`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/Terarria1456/Terraria/Player.cs:41215-41234`
```csharp
	private void PlaceThing_PaintScrapper_TryScrapping(int x, int y)
	{
		Tile tile = Main.tile[x, y];
		if ((0u | ((tile.wall > 0 && (tile.wallColor() > 0 || tile.invisibleWall() || tile.fullbrightWall())) ? 1u : 0u) | ((tile.active() && (tile.color() > 0 || tile.invisibleBlock() || tile.fullbrightBlock())) ? 1u : 0u)) == 0)
		{
			return;
		}
		cursorItemIconEnabled = true;
		if (ItemTimeIsZero && itemAnimation > 0 && controlUseItem)
		{
			if (WorldGen.paintTile(x, y, 0, broadCast: true) || WorldGen.paintCoatTile(x, y, 0, broadcast: true))
			{
				ApplyItemTime(inventory[selectedItem], tileSpeed);
			}
			else if (WorldGen.paintWall(x, y, 0, broadCast: true) || WorldGen.paintCoatWall(x, y, 0, broadcast: true))
			{
				ApplyItemTime(inventory[selectedItem], wallSpeed);
			}
		}
	}
```
语义：只有该格"有墙色/墙涂层"或"有方块色/方块涂层"时才显示图标并动作；清除时**优先 tile 通道**（`paintTile(x,y,0)` + `paintCoatTile(x,y,0)`），tile 通道没成功才清墙。刮刀**不消耗任何物品**（本方法没有 `stack--`）。

`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/Terarria1456/Terraria/Player.cs:41166-41213`（长苔藓 Long Moss，tile type 184）
```csharp
	private void PlaceThing_PaintScrapper_LongMoss(int x, int y)
	{
		if (Main.tile[x, y].type != 184)
		{
			return;
		}
		cursorItemIconEnabled = true;
		if (!ItemTimeIsZero || itemAnimation <= 0 || !controlUseItem)
		{
			return;
		}
		_ = Main.tile[x, y].type;
		int frameX = Main.tile[x, y].frameX;
		WorldGen.KillTile(x, y);
		if (Main.tile[x, y].active())
		{
			return;
		}
		ApplyItemTime(inventory[selectedItem]);
		if (Main.netMode == 1)
		{
			NetMessage.SendData(17, -1, -1, null, 0, x, y);
		}
		if (Main.rand.Next(9) == 0)
		{
			int type = 4349 + frameX / 22;
			switch (frameX / 22)
			{
			case 6:
				type = 4377;
				break;
			case 7:
				type = 4378;
				break;
			case 8:
				type = 4389;
				break;
			case 9:
				type = 5127;
				break;
			case 10:
				type = 5128;
				break;
			}
			int number = Item.NewItem(WorldGen.GetItemSource_FromTileBreak(x, y), x * 16, y * 16, 16, 16, type);
			NetMessage.SendData(21, -1, -1, null, number, 1f);
		}
	}
```
语义：刮刀对长苔藓(184)还有隐藏功能——直接 `KillTile` 并以 1/9 概率按 `frameX/22` 掉对应苔藓物品（对应 tooltip "Can sometimes collect moss"）。SmartSelect 也会为此自动选刮刀：`Player.cs:17504-17508` `else if (type == 184 && IsInTileInteractionRange(..., paintScraperRange)) { toolStrategy = 8; ... }`。

刮刀判定集合 `~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/Terarria1456/Terraria.ID/ItemID.cs:254`
```csharp
		public static bool[] IsPaintScraper = Factory.CreateBoolSet(1100, 1545);
```

### 1.4 (d) tile / wall 两通道的真正区分方式

通道由**工具类型 id** 决定，通过 `TryPainting` 的 `paintingAWall` 布尔参数传入；与物品 `paint` 字段、`controlUseTile` 无关。

`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/Terarria1456/Terraria/Player.cs:41272-41305`
```csharp
	public Item FindPaintOrCoating()
	{
		for (int i = 54; i < 58; i++)
		{
			if (inventory[i].stack > 0 && inventory[i].PaintOrCoating)
			{
				return inventory[i];
			}
		}
		for (int j = 0; j < 58; j++)
		{
			if (inventory[j].stack > 0 && inventory[j].PaintOrCoating)
			{
				return inventory[j];
			}
		}
		return null;
	}

	private void TryPainting(int x, int y, bool paintingAWall = false, bool applyItemAnimation = true)
	{
		Item item = FindPaintOrCoating();
		if (item != null)
		{
			if (item.paintCoating != 0)
			{
				ApplyCoating(x, y, paintingAWall, applyItemAnimation, item);
			}
			if (item.paint != 0)
			{
				ApplyPaint(x, y, paintingAWall, applyItemAnimation, item);
			}
		}
	}
```
语义：涂什么由背包里找到的**油漆/涂层物品**决定（优先弹药栏 54-57，再全背包），涂到 tile 还是 wall 由工具（刷子/滚刷）通过 `paintingAWall` 决定。

Item 字段定义与判定属性：

`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/Terarria1456/Terraria/Item.cs:117-121`
```csharp
	public byte dye;

	public byte paint;

	public byte paintCoating;
```
（行号精确为 117 `dye`、118 `paint`、119 空行、120 `paintCoating`。）

`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/Terarria1456/Terraria/Item.cs:334-344`
```csharp
	public bool PaintOrCoating
	{
		get
		{
			if (paint <= 0)
			{
				return paintCoating > 0;
			}
			return true;
		}
	}
```
重置处 `~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/Terarria1456/Terraria/Item.cs:48562-48563`：`paint = 0;` / `paintCoating = 0;`

底层两通道存储（Tile 两个 5bit 颜色位域）：
`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/Terarria1456/Terraria/Tile.cs:609-617`（方块色，`sTileHeader` 低 5 位）
```csharp
	public byte color()
	{
		return (byte)(sTileHeader & 0x1F);
	}

	public void color(byte color)
	{
		sTileHeader = (ushort)((sTileHeader & 0xFFE0) | color);
	}
```
`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/Terarria1456/Terraria/Tile.cs:365-373`（墙色，`bTileHeader` 低 5 位）
```csharp
	public byte wallColor()
	{
		return (byte)(bTileHeader & 0x1F);
	}

	public void wallColor(byte wallColor)
	{
		bTileHeader = (byte)((bTileHeader & 0xE0) | wallColor);
	}
```
语义：tile 色和 wall 色是同一 Tile 对象里两个独立位域，涂刷/刮除分别写它们。

WorldGen 四个通道原语（全部位于 `~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/Terarria1456/Terraria/WorldGen.cs`）：
- `paintTile` 44137-44159、`paintCoatTile` 44161-44207（tile 通道，packet 63）
- `paintCoatWall` 44209-44255、`paintWall` 44257-44279（wall 通道，packet 64）

```csharp
	public static bool paintWall(int x, int y, byte color, bool broadCast = false, bool paintEffects = true)
	{
		Tile tile = Main.tile[x, y];
		if (tile == null || tile.wall == 0)
		{
			return false;
		}
		byte b = tile.wallColor();
		if (b == color)
		{
			return false;
		}
		tile.wallColor(color);
		if (broadCast)
		{
			NetMessage.SendData(64, -1, -1, null, x, y, (int)color);
		}
		if (paintEffects)
		{
			paintEffect(x, y, color, b);
		}
		return true;
	}
```
（`WorldGen.cs:44257-44279`）

```

"GameUI.PaintSprayerOn" ...)`)。语义：装备喷漆器且未关闭时，放置方块/墙会自动额外消耗油漆涂上（`applyItemAnimation: false`，不重置 itemTime）。

## 2. 消耗语义

### 2.1 每次成功涂一格扣 1 个油漆物品

`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/Terarria1456/Terraria/Player.cs:41339-41369`（ApplyPaint 完整原文）
```csharp
	private void ApplyPaint(int x, int y, bool paintingAWall, bool applyItemAnimation, Item targetItem)
	{
		byte paint = targetItem.paint;
		if (paintingAWall)
		{
			if (Main.tile[x, y].wallColor() != paint && WorldGen.paintWall(x, y, paint, broadCast: true))
			{
				targetItem.stack--;
				if (targetItem.stack <= 0)
				{
					targetItem.SetDefaults(0);
				}
				if (applyItemAnimation)
				{
					ApplyItemTime(inventory[selectedItem], wallSpeed);
				}
			}
		}
		else if (Main.tile[x, y].color() != paint && WorldGen.paintTile(x, y, paint, broadCast: true))
		{
			targetItem.stack--;
			if (targetItem.stack <= 0)
			{
				targetItem.SetDefaults(0);
			}
			if (applyItemAnimation)
			{
				ApplyItemTime(inventory[selectedItem], tileSpeed);
			}
		}
	}
```

`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/Terarria1456/Terraria/Player.cs:41307-41337`（ApplyCoating 完整原文）
```csharp
	private void ApplyCoating(int x, int y, bool paintingAWall, bool applyItemAnimation, Item targetItem)
	{
		byte paintCoating = targetItem.paintCoating;
		if (paintingAWall)
		{
			if (WorldGen.paintCoatWall(x, y, paintCoating, broadcast: true))
			{
				targetItem.stack--;
				if (targetItem.stack <= 0)
				{
					targetItem.SetDefaults(0);
				}
				if (applyItemAnimation)
				{
					ApplyItemTime(inventory[selectedItem], wallSpeed);
				}
			}
		}
		else if (WorldGen.paintCoatTile(x, y, paintCoating, broadcast: true))
		{
			targetItem.stack--;
			if (targetItem.stack <= 0)
			{
				targetItem.SetDefaults(0);
			}
			if (applyItemAnimation)
			{
				ApplyItemTime(inventory[selectedItem], tileSpeed);
			}
		}
	}
```
语义：**涂一次（一格）扣 1 个**，且仅当 `WorldGen.paintXxx` 返回 true（颜色真的变了）才扣；把已同色格再刷一遍**不消耗**（`ApplyPaint` 里 `Main.tile[x, y].color() != paint` 的前置守卫 + `WorldGen.paintTile:44145-44148` 的 `if (b == color) return false;`）。涂层同理（`paintCoatTile:44183-44186` `if (tile.fullbrightBlock()) return false;`）。

### 2.2 滚刷也是每次 1 个，不是一排扣 N 个

`Player.cs:41249` `TryPainting(num, num2, paintingAWall: true);` —— 滚刷每次 itemTime 触发只处理光标单格，走与刷子完全相同的 `ApplyPaint/ApplyCoating`，因此**每次扣 1 个**（一堵墙 N 格就是 N 次 × 1 个）。

### 2.3 没有 TryConsumingPaintQuantity / 不走 HasAmmo / PickAmmo

`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/Terarria1456/Terraria/Player.cs:52562-52577`
```csharp
	public bool HasAmmo(Item sItem, bool canUse)
	{
		if (sItem.useAmmo > 0)
		{
			canUse = false;
			for (int i = 0; i < 58; i++)
			{
				if (inventory[i].ammo == sItem.useAmmo && inventory[i].stack > 0)
				{
					canUse = true;
					break;
				}
			}
		}
		return canUse;
	}
```
`PickAmmo` 在 `Player.cs:52589` 起，全按 `inventory[j].ammo == sItem.useAmmo` 匹配（52594-52629）。油漆工具 `useAmmo` 均为默认 `AmmoID.None`（`Item.cs:234` `public int useAmmo = AmmoID.None;`），**完全不经过弹药系统**；等效"找弹药"的是 `FindPaintOrCoating`（`Player.cs:41272-41289`，先弹药栏 54-57 再 0-57，按 `Item.PaintOrCoating` 匹配）。Player.cs 中不存在 `TryConsumingPaintQuantity`。

### 2.4 油漆可放入弹药栏

`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/Terarria1456/Terraria/Item.cs:1351-1367`
```csharp
	public bool FitsAmmoSlot()
	{
		if ((type != 0 && ammo <= 0 && bait <= 0 && type != 530 && type != 849 && !PaintOrCoating) || notAmmo)
		{
			return type == 353;
		}
		return true;
	}

	public bool CanFillEmptyAmmoSlot()
	{
		if (bait <= 0 && !PaintOrCoating && type != 353 && type != 849 && type != 169 && type != 75 && type != 23 && type != 408 && type != 370 && type != 1246)
		{
			return !notAmmo;
		}
		return false;
	}
```
语义：`PaintOrCoating` 为 true 的物品被显式允许进弹药槽（`CanFillEmptyAmmoSlot` 对其返回 false 表示不自动填，但 `FitsAmmoSlot` 返回 true 允许放入）。

### 2.5 itemTime 与速度乘子

`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/Terarria1456/Terraria/Player.cs:4216-4230`
```csharp
	public void ApplyItemTime(Item sItem)
	{
		SetItemTime(sItem.useTime);
	}

	public void ApplyItemTime(Item sItem, float multiplier)
	{
		int useTime = sItem.useTime;
		int val = (int)((float)useTime * multiplier);
		if (useTime > 0)
		{
			val = Math.Max(val, 1);
		}
		SetItemTime(val);
	}
```
字段 `Player.cs:2336` `public float wallSpeed = 1f;` / `2338` `public float tileSpeed = 1f;`（每帧在 ResetEffects 复位 `Player.cs:18364-18365`）。门控属性 `Player.cs:3902` `public bool ItemTimeIsZero => itemTime == 0;`。语义：涂刷冷却 = `useTime(10) × tileSpeed或wallSpeed`，所以 Toolbelt/手 Of Creation 等也会加速涂刷。

---

## 3. 哪些 tile 可涂 / 不可涂

**结论：本源码中不存在按 tile 类型的涂刷黑名单（没有 CanPaintTile 这类函数，树、箱子、门、画框全部可涂）。可涂判定只有 4 条：**

1. **刷子（tile 通道）**：目标格必须 active——`Player.cs:41262`
```csharp
		if (Main.tile[num, num2] != null && Main.tile[num, num2].active())
```
底层 `WorldGen.paintTile` 的守卫——`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/Terarria1456/Terraria/WorldGen.cs:44137-44148`
```csharp
	public static bool paintTile(int x, int y, byte color, bool broadCast = false, bool paintEffects = true)
	{
		Tile tile = Main.tile[x, y];
		if (tile == null || !tile.active())
		{
			return false;
		}
		byte b = tile.color();
		if (b == color)
		{
			return false;
		}
```
即"非 active 的空气格"不可涂；同色重复涂视为无效。

2. **滚刷（wall 通道）**：目标格必须有墙——`Player.cs:41244`
```csharp
		if (Main.tile[num, num2] != null && Main.tile[num, num2].wall > 0)
```
底层 `WorldGen.cs:44259-44263`
```csharp
		Tile tile = Main.tile[x, y];
		if (tile == null || tile.wall == 0)
		{
			return false;
		}
```
注意：**没有**"墙前有实心方块就不可涂"的检查——滚刷可以把实心砖后面的墙涂掉（Player 层无遮挡判断）。

3. **刮刀**：只有"当前有色/有涂层"才可刮——`Player.cs:41218`
```csharp
		if ((0u | ((tile.wall > 0 && (tile.wallColor() > 0 || tile.invisibleWall() || tile.fullbrightWall())) ? 1u : 0u) | ((tile.active() && (tile.color() > 0 || tile.invisibleBlock() || tile.fullbrightBlock())) ? 1u : 0u)) == 0)
		{
			return;
		}
```

4. **SmartCursor 对滚刷额外要求"墙可见"**（仅影响智能光标选点，不影响实际涂刷）——`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/Terarria1456/Terraria.GameContent/SmartCursorHelper.cs:781`
```csharp
					if (tile.wall > 0 && (!tile.active() || !Main.tileSolid[tile.type] || Main.tileSolidTop[tile.type]) && (0u | ((paintLookup != 0 && tile.wallColor() != paintLookup) ? 1u : 0u) | ((paintCoatingLookup == 1 && !tile.fullbrightWall()) ? 1u : 0u) | ((paintCoatingLookup == 2 && !tile.invisibleWall()) ? 1u : 0u)) != 0)
```
语义：智能光标只会把光标指到"有墙且前面没有实心方块"的格子。

多层对象（箱子/门/树）的特殊性：涂刷只改**被点中那一格**的 `tile.color()`（`WorldGen.cs:44153` `tile.color(color);`），不会扩散到整个多格物体——这就是"涂箱子只涂到一格"的根源，源码里没有例外分支。

网络同步（涂刷结果广播）——`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/Terarria1456/Terraria/MessageBuffer.cs:2920-2959`
```csharp
		case 63:
		{
			int num193 = reader.ReadInt16();
			int num194 = reader.ReadInt16();
			byte b12 = reader.ReadByte();
			byte b13 = reader.ReadByte();
			if (b13 == 0)
			{
				WorldGen.paintTile(num193, num194, b12);
			}
			else
			{
				WorldGen.paintCoatTile(num193, num194, b12);
			}
			if (Main.netMode == 2)
			{
				NetMessage.TrySendData(63, -1, whoAmI, null, num193, num194, (int)b12, (int)b13);
			}
			break;
		}
		case 64:
		{
			int num173 = reader.ReadInt16();
			int num174 = reader.ReadInt16();
			byte b10 = reader.ReadByte();
			byte b11 = reader.ReadByte();
			if (b11 == 0)
			{
				WorldGen.paintWall(num173, num174, b10);
			}
			else
			{
				WorldGen.paintCoatWall(num173, num174, b10);
			}
			if (Main.netMode == 2)
			{
				NetMessage.TrySendData(64, -1, whoAmI, null, num173, num174, (int)b10, (int)b11);
			}
			break;
		}
```
消息名 `~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/Terarria1456/Terraria.ID/MessageID.cs:139` `public const byte SyncTilePaintOrCoating = 63;`、`141` `public const byte SyncWallPaintOrCoating = 64;`。第 4 个字节为 coating 标志（0=普通色，1=涂层）。服务器端收到后直接调 WorldGen.paint*，**没有范围/类型校验**。

## 4. SmartCursorHelper 的三个油漆策略

文件：`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/Terarria1456/Terraria.GameContent/SmartCursorHelper.cs`

注册与查找（`SmartCursorHelper.cs:93` 先查油漆，`146-148` 依次跑 Roller→Brush→Scrapper）：
```csharp
		TryFindingPaintInplayerInventory(smartCursorUsageInfo, out smartCursorUsageInfo.paintLookup, out smartCursorUsageInfo.paintCoatingLookup);
```
```csharp
			Step_PaintRoller(smartCursorUsageInfo, ref fX, ref fY);
			Step_PaintBrush(smartCursorUsageInfo, ref fX, ref fY);
			Step_PaintScrapper(smartCursorUsageInfo, ref fX, ref fY);
```

`SmartCursorHelper.cs:167-181`（从背包读出当前要涂的颜色/涂层）
```csharp
	private static void TryFindingPaintInplayerInventory(SmartCursorUsageInfo providedInfo, out int paintLookup, out int coatingLookup)
	{
		_ = providedInfo.player.inventory;
		paintLookup = 0;
		coatingLookup = 0;
		if (providedInfo.item.type == 1071 || providedInfo.item.type == 1543 || providedInfo.item.type == 1072 || providedInfo.item.type == 1544)
		{
			Item item = providedInfo.player.FindPaintOrCoating();
			if (item != null)
			{
				coatingLookup = item.paintCoating;
				paintLookup = item.paint;
			}
		}
	}
```
承载字段 `SmartCursorHelper.cs:38-40`：`public int paintLookup;` / `public int paintCoatingLookup;`

### 4.1 Step_PaintScrapper（`SmartCursorHelper.cs:668-720`）
```csharp
	private static void Step_PaintScrapper(SmartCursorUsageInfo providedInfo, ref int focusedX, ref int focusedY)
	{
		if (!ItemID.Sets.IsPaintScraper[providedInfo.item.type] || focusedX != -1 || focusedY != -1)
		{
			return;
		}
		_targets.Clear();
		for (int i = providedInfo.reachableStartX; i <= providedInfo.reachableEndX; i++)
		{
			for (int j = providedInfo.reachableStartY; j <= providedInfo.reachableEndY; j++)
			{
				Tile tile = Main.tile[i, j];
				bool flag = false;
				if (tile.active())
				{
					flag |= tile.color() > 0;
					flag |= tile.type == 184;
					flag |= tile.fullbrightBlock();
					flag |= tile.invisibleBlock();
				}
				if (tile.wall > 0)
				{
					flag |= tile.wallColor() > 0;
					flag |= tile.fullbrightWall();
					flag |= tile.invisibleWall();
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					_targets.Add(new Point(i, j));
				}
			}
		}
		if (_targets.Count > 0)
		{
			float num = -1f;
			Point point = _targets[0];
			for (int k = 0; k < _targets.Count; k++)
			{
				float num2 = Vector2.Distance(new Vector2(_targets[k].X, _targets[k].Y) * 16f + Vector2.One * 8f, providedInfo.mouse);
				if (num == -1f || num2 < num)
				{
					num = num2;
					point = _targets[k];
				}
			}
			if (Collision.InTileBounds(point.X, point.Y, providedInfo.reachableStartX, providedInfo.reachableStartY, providedInfo.reachableEndX, providedInfo.reachableEndY))
			{
				focusedX = point.X;
				focusedY = point.Y;
			}
		}
		_targets.Clear();
	}
```
语义：扫描整个可及矩形，把"有方块色/墙色/任一涂层/是长苔藓(184)"的格子都算目标，选**离鼠标最近**的一个。

### 4.2 Step_PaintBrush（`SmartCursorHelper.cs:722-765`）
```csharp
	private static void Step_PaintBrush(SmartCursorUsageInfo providedInfo, ref int focusedX, ref int focusedY)
	{
		if ((providedInfo.item.type != 1071 && providedInfo.item.type != 1543) || (providedInfo.paintLookup == 0 && providedInfo.paintCoatingLookup == 0) || focusedX != -1 || focusedY != -1)
		{
			return;
		}
		_targets.Clear();
		int paintLookup = providedInfo.paintLookup;
		int paintCoatingLookup = providedInfo.paintCoatingLookup;
		if (paintLookup != 0 || paintCoatingLookup != 0)
		{
			for (int i = providedInfo.reachableStartX; i <= providedInfo.reachableEndX; i++)
			{
				for (int j = providedInfo.reachableStartY; j <= providedInfo.reachableEndY; j++)
				{
					Tile tile = Main.tile[i, j];
					if (tile.active() && (0u | ((paintLookup != 0 && tile.color() != paintLookup) ? 1u : 0u) | ((paintCoatingLookup == 1 && !tile.fullbrightBlock()) ? 1u : 0u) | ((paintCoatingLookup == 2 && !tile.invisibleBlock()) ? 1u : 0u)) != 0)
					{
						_targets.Add(new Point(i, j));
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if (_targets.Count > 0)
		{
			float num = -1f;
			Point point = _targets[0];
			for (int k = 0; k < _targets.Count; k++)
			{
				float num2 = Vector2.Distance(new Vector2(_targets[k].X, _targets[k].Y) * 16f + Vector2.One * 8f, providedInfo.mouse);
				if (num == -1f || num2 < num)
				{
					num = num2;
					point = _targets[k];
				}
			}
			if (Collision.InTileBounds(point.X, point.Y, providedInfo.reachableStartX, providedInfo.reachableStartY, providedInfo.reachableEndX, providedInfo.reachableEndY))
			{
				focusedX = point.X;
				focusedY = point.Y;
			}
		}
		_targets.Clear();
	}
```
语义：只在"身上真的有油漆/涂层"时启用；目标是所有"active 且（颜色不等于目标色 或 缺 Illuminant/Echo 涂层）"的格子，取离鼠标最近者。注意目标条件里**没有按 tile 类型过滤**。

### 4.3 Step_PaintRoller（`SmartCursorHelper.cs:767-807`）
```csharp
	private static void Step_PaintRoller(SmartCursorUsageInfo providedInfo, ref int focusedX, ref int focusedY)
	{
		if ((providedInfo.item.type != 1072 && providedInfo.item.type != 1544) || (providedInfo.paintLookup == 0 && providedInfo.paintCoatingLookup == 0) || focusedX != -1 || focusedY != -1)
		{
			return;
		}
		_targets.Clear();
		int paintLookup = providedInfo.paintLookup;
		int paintCoatingLookup = providedInfo.paintCoatingLookup;
		for (int i = providedInfo.reachableStartX; i <= providedInfo.reachableEndX; i++)
		{
			for (int j = providedInfo.reachableStartY; j <= providedInfo.reachableEndY; j++)
			{
				Tile tile = Main.tile[i, j];
				if (tile.wall > 0 && (!tile.active() || !Main.tileSolid[tile.type] || Main.tileSolidTop[tile.type]) && (0u | ((paintLookup != 0 && tile.wallColor() != paintLookup) ? 1u : 0u) | ((paintCoatingLookup == 1 && !tile.fullbrightWall()) ? 1u : 0u) | ((paintCoatingLookup == 2 && !tile.invisibleWall()) ? 1u : 0u)) != 0)
				{
					_targets.Add(new Point(i, j));
				}
			}
		}
		if (_targets.Count > 0)
		{
			float num = -1f;
			Point point = _targets[0];
			for (int k = 0; k < _targets.Count; k++)
			{
				float num2 = Vector2.Distance(new Vector2(_targets[k].X, _targets[k].Y) * 16f + Vector2.One * 8f, providedInfo.mouse);
				if (num == -1f || num2 < num)
				{
					num = num2;
					point = _targets[k];
				}
			}
			if (Collision.InTileBounds(point.X, point.Y, providedInfo.reachableStartX, providedInfo.reachableStartY, providedInfo.reachableEndX, providedInfo.reachableEndY))
			{
				focusedX = point.X;
				focusedY = point.Y;
			}
		}
		_targets.Clear();
	}
```
语义：滚刷智能光标目标是"有墙、墙未被实心方块完全遮住、且墙色/涂层与目标不一致"的格子，取离鼠标最近。三策略共同的收尾都是"最近点 + `Collision.InTileBounds` 校验后写入 `focusedX/focusedY`"，随后在 `SmartCursorLookup`（`SmartCursorHelper.cs:157-162`）把 `Main.SmartCursorX/Y` 与 `Player.tileTargetX/Y` 一并改写，从而让 `PlaceThing_Paintbrush/PaintRoller` 涂到智能光标点。

## 一句话总览

涂刷 = `ItemCheck → PlaceThing`（`Player.cs:38923-38925`）里三个按"手持物品 id / ItemID.Sets.IsPaintScraper"分派的分支；刷子(1071/1543)只认 `tile.active()` 走 tile 通道，滚刷(1072/1544)只认 `tile.wall > 0` 走 wall 通道（一次都只涂光标一格、成功才扣 1 个油漆/涂层），刮刀(1100/1545)先清 tile 再清墙且零消耗、附带剃长苔藓；可涂性判定里没有任何按 tile 类型的黑名单；SmartCursor 三策略（`SmartCursorHelper.cs:668-807`）只是从可及矩形里挑"离鼠标最近、且确实需要涂/刮"的一格并改写 `tileTargetX/Y`。
````
